def calculate_premiums_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """"""
    4200-calculate_premiums"""
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ insurance_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                    PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                    PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
            
        
    """"""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    main_data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not main_data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading insurance_master next
        # In a real scenario, you would read from a file or database
        insurance_master_record = InsuranceMaster()  # Assuming a new record each time

        if insurance_master_record is None:  # Simulate AT END
            main_data.WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            determine_base_premium_C2(main_data)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(main_data)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(main_data)

def determine_base_premium_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    match True:
        case main_data.INS_LIFE:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * main_data.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case main_data.INS_HEALTH:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case main_data.INS_AUTO:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case main_data.INS_HOME:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * main_data.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case main_data.INS_UMBRELLA:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
        case _:
            pass

def apply_risk_factor_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if main_data.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    # Assuming INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT is a property of insurance_master_record
    # insurance_master_record.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    main_data.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT #correct way
    main_data.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_position_value_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    main_data.INV_MARKET_VALUE = main_data.INV_QUANTITY * main_data.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    main_data.INV_GAIN_LOSS = main_data.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (main_data.INV_QUANTITY * main_data.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def calculate_dividends_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:"""
    """"""
    5400-calculate_dividends"""
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ investment_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    IF inv_dividend_rate > 0
                        PERFORM 5410-compute_dividend
                        PERFORM 5420-post_dividend
                    
            
        
    """"""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    main_data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not main_data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading investment_master next
        # In a real scenario, you would read from a file or database

                compute_dividend_C2(main_data)
                post_dividend_C2(main_data)

    main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.INV_MARKET_VALUE * main_data.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

def daily_summary_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """"""
    6100-daily_summary"""
        DISPLAY "GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY..."
        MOVE SPACES TO report_line
        STRING "mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_current_date DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        PERFORM 6110-write_totals
    """"""
    print("GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY...")
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = ""
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = f"mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - {main_data.WS_CURRENT_DATE}"
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)
    write_totals_C2(main_data)

def write_totals_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """"""
    6110-write_totals"""
        MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount"""
        STRING "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_loans TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL LOANS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
    """"""
    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)"""
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL DEPOSITS: {main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_LOANS)
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL LOANS: {main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

def format_date_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """
    8300-format_date
        STRING ws_temp_date(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_temp_date(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE"""
               ws_temp_date(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE"""

    """"""

        main_data.WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2(main_data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    match True:
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = (main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                     ((main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = (main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                     ((main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                                     ((main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * main_data.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Account:
    ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Loan:
    LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool = False
    
WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_DOMESTIC: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_INTL: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
WS_EOF: bool = False
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
WS_CURRENT_DATE: int = 0

CUSTOMER_MASTER = CustomerMaster()
ACCT_BALANCE = Decimal("0.00")
ACCT_MIN_BALANCE = Decimal("0.00")
LOAN_DELINQUENT = False

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulating READ customer_master NEXT
        # In a real implementation, this would read from a data source
        # For this example, we are just simulating the loop
        # Assuming there is a function to get the next customer
        customer = get_next_customer() 
        if customer is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_clv_C3(customer)
            assign_segment_C3()

def calculate_clv_C3(customer: CustomerMaster) -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (customer.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                      (customer.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                      (customer.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE, WS_CALC_RESULT
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """9632-sweep_accounts."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCT_BALANCE, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def extract_data_A110_C3() -> None:
    """A110-extract_data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_PROCESS_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulating READ customer_master NEXT
        # In a real implementation, this would read from a data source
        # For this example, we are just simulating the loop
        customer = get_next_customer() 
        if customer is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

class LoanRecord:
    """Represents the Loan Record data structure."""
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: bool = False

cust_status: str = ""
ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
cust_credit_score: int = 0
cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_risk_rating: str = ""
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
ws_temp_string: str = ""
ws_valid: bool = False
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_formatted_count: str = ""
ws_cust_count: int = 0
transaction_log: TransactionLog = TransactionLog()
loan_record: LoanRecord = LoanRecord()
cust_ssn: str = ""

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """C100-transaction_monitoring"""
    DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ transaction_log NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM C110-rule_based_detection
                PERFORM C120-behavior_analysis
                PERFORM C130-network_analysis
        
    
    """"""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, transaction_log"""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False  # Ensure ws_eof is initially False

    while not ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading the next transaction (replace with actual file reading)
        # For demonstration, create a dummy transaction
        transaction_log = TransactionLog(tran_amount=Decimal("7500.00"))  # Example

        if transaction_log:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
            ws_eof = True # stop
        else:
            ws_eof = True  # Simulate AT END

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if transaction_log.tran_amount is not None:
        if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:
            c111_flag_ctr_C4()
        if 5000 <= transaction_log.tran_amount < 10000:
            c112_check_structuring_C4()

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global cust_risk_rating
    if cust_credit_score > 750:
        cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif cust_credit_score > 650:
        cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif cust_credit_score > 550:
        cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = Decimal((cust_credit_score * 10) + 0  # TODO
                            (cust_total_balance / Decimal("1000.00")) - 0  # TODO
                            (cust_total_loans / Decimal("2000.00")))

def main_loop_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ customer_master NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM I110-update_profile
                PERFORM I120-enrich_profile
                ADD 1 TO ws_cust_count
        
    
    """
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_CUST_COUNT

    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            customer_master = CUSTOMER_FILE.pop(0)  # Simulate reading from file
            i110_update_profile_C5()
            i120_enrich_profile_C5()
            WS_CUST_COUNT += 1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF = True

def _1000_initialization_C5() -> None:"""
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, WS_COUNTERS, WS_TOTALS, WS_CURRENT_DATETIME, RPT_YEAR, RPT_MONTH, RPT_DAY
    WS_WORK_AREAS = {}
    WS_COUNTERS = {}
    WS_TOTALS = {}
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = datetime.now().isoformat()
    RPT_YEAR = datetime.now().year
    RPT_MONTH = datetime.now().month
    RPT_DAY = datetime.now().day
    _1100_open_files_C5()
    _1200_read_parameters_C5()
    _1300_initialize_tables_C5()
    _1400_load_reference_data_C5()

def _1100_open_files_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG

    # In Python, we don't explicitly "open" files in the same way as COBOL'
    # We'll assume the files are "opened" by simply referencing them'
    #CUSTOMER_FILE = open("customer_file.txt", "r") # Example for a text file
    #ACCOUNT_FILE = open("account_file.txt", "r") # Example for a text file
    #TRANSACTION_FILE = open("transaction_file.txt", "r") # Example for a text file
    #REPORT_FILE = open("report_file.txt", "w") # Example for a text file
    #ERROR_FILE = open("error_file.txt", "w") # Example for a text file
    #MASTER_FILE = open("master_file.dat", "r+b") # Example for a binary file in read/write mode

    WS_FILE_STATUS = '00' #Assuming no error
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        _9500_abort_process_C5()

def _1200_read_parameters_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ACCEPT ws_param_time FROM TIME
    MOVE 'batch_001' TO ws_job_id
    MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO ws_env_type
    COMPUTE ws_process_date = 
       FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_param_date)
    """"""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE"""
""""""
    WS_PARAM_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_PARAM_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(WS_PARAM_DATE) #Simulating integer_of_date

def _1300_initialize_tables_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
       UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 100
       INITIALIZE rate_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)
       MOVE ZEROES TO rt_rate(ws_tbl_idx)
       MOVE SPACES TO rt_code(ws_tbl_idx)
    
    PERFORM VARYING ws_tbl_idx FROM 1 BY 1
       UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 50
       INITIALIZE branch_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)
    
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, REFERENCE_FILE, WS_REF_RECORD, RT_CODE, RT_RATE, WS_REF_CODE, WS_REF_RATE

    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        try:
            WS_REF_RECORD = REFERENCE_FILE.pop(0) #Simulate reading from file
            WS_REF_CODE = WS_REF_RECORD.get("code", "") # Assuming REFERENCE_FILE contains dictionaries with 'code' and 'rate'
            WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(str(WS_REF_RECORD.get("rate", "0.00"))) #Convert to Decimal, handle missing key
            RT_CODE[WS_TBL_IDX]  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_CODE
            RT_RATE[WS_TBL_IDX]  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_RATE
            WS_TBL_IDX += 1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def _2000_process_transactions_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
       AT END
          MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
       NOT AT END
          ADD 1 TO ws_trans_count
          PERFORM 2100-validate_transaction
          IF ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
             PERFORM 2200-process_by_type

             PERFORM 2900-handle_error
          
    
    """
    global WS_TRANSACTION_REC, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_VALID_FLAG
"""
    try:"""
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC = TRANSACTION_FILE.pop(0)  # Simulate reading from file"""
        TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.get("TXN_ACCOUNT_ID", "")
        TXN_AMOUNT = Decimal(str(WS_TRANSACTION_REC.get("TXN_AMOUNT", "0.00")))
        TXN_TYPE = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.get("TXN_TYPE", "")
        TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.get("TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT", "")

        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
        _2100_validate_transaction_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            _2200_process_by_type_C5()
        else:
            _2900_handle_error_C5()
    except IndexError:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def _2100_validate_transaction_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    IF txn_account_id = SPACES OR low_values
       MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
       MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO ws_error_msg
       EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    IF txn_amount IS NOT NUMERIC
       MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
       MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO ws_error_msg
       EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    IF txn_type NOT = 'D' AND txn_type NOT = 'W'
       AND txn_type NOT = 'T' AND txn_type NOT = 'I'
       MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
       MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO ws_error_msg
    
    PERFORM 2150-validate_account_exists
    PERFORM 2160-validate_business_rules
    """
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE"""
""""""
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'"""
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == chr(0): #SPACES and low_values in Python
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT) #Check if numeric
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    if TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'

    _2150_validate_account_exists_C5()
    _2160_validate_business_rules_C5()

def _2150_validate_account_exists_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    PERFORM 5000-search_account
    IF ws_found_flag = 'N'
       MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
       MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO ws_error_msg
    
    """
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID

    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    _5000_search_account_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'"""
""""""
def _2160_validate_business_rules_C5() -> None:"""
    """COBOL logic"""
    global TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG

    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'

    if TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal('1000000.00'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def _2200_process_by_type_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global TXN_TYPE

    match TXN_TYPE:
        case 'D':
            _2300_process_deposit_C5()
        case 'W':
            _2400_process_withdrawal_C5()
        case 'T':
            _2500_process_transfer_C5()
        case 'I':
            _2600_process_interest_C5()
        case _:
            _2900_handle_error_C5()

def _2300_process_deposit_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO ws_txn_desc
    ADD txn_amount TO ws_total_deposits
    ADD 1 TO ws_deposit_count
    PERFORM 2350-update_account
    PERFORM 2380-write_audit_trail
    """
    global TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1
    _2350_update_account_C5()
    _2380_write_audit_trail_C5()

def _2350_update_account_C5() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO acct_last_update
    REWRITE account_record
    IF ws_file_status NOT = '00'
       MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO ws_error_msg

def calculate_interest_rate_C6(interest_type: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    WHEN OTHER
        MOVE 2.5 TO ws_interest_rate
    
    global WS_INTEREST_RATE
    match interest_type:
        case "A":
            WS_INTEREST_RATE = Decimal('1.5')
        case "B":
            WS_INTEREST_RATE = Decimal('2.0')
        case _:
            WS_INTEREST_RATE = Decimal('2.5')

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE * Decimal(WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD) / Decimal('36500')

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""COBOL: AmortRecord"""
    amort_interest: list[Decimal]
    amort_principal: list[Decimal]
    amort_balance: list[Decimal]
    amort_payment_num: list[int]
    amort_payment_amt: list[Decimal]
    amort_escrow: list[Decimal]
    amort_total_pmt: list[Decimal]
    amort_payment_date: list[int]

    def calculate_pmi_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10335-calculate_pmi."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
            case _:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0050") / 12

    def evaluate_history_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10340-evaluate_history."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    def calculate_final_risk_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10350-calculate_final_risk."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE // 4
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
            case _:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

    def determine_approval_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10400-determine_approval."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
            return
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
            return
        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
            return
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
        self.calculate_approved_terms_C7()

    def calculate_approved_terms_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10450-calculate_approved_terms."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case 'A':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.00")
            case 'B':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.50")
            case 'C':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("1.50")
            case 'D':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("3.00")
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal("0.50")

    def generate_loan_terms_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10500-generate_loan_terms."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (
            self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
            * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
            * self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR
            / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

    def create_amortization_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10600-create_amortization."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # FUNCTION current_date
        self.WS_AMORT_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_AMORT_IDX <= self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS:
            self.calculate_payment_split_C7()
            self.WS_AMORT_IDX += 1

    def calculate_payment_split_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10650-calculate_payment_split."""
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        )
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
        )
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        if self.LOAN_MORTGAGE:
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
                self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM
            ) / 12
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
                + self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
                + self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT
            )
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.advance_payment_date_C7()

    def advance_payment_date_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10660-advance_payment_date."""
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1
        )

    def finalize_loan_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10700-finalize_loan."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # FUNCTION current_date
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = (
            self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
        self.create_loan_record_C7()
        self.disburse_funds_C7()
        self.send_confirmation_C7()

    def create_loan_record_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10750-create_loan_record."""
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD = WSLoanRecordC7()
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_type = self.WS_LOAN_TYPE
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_amount = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_rate = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_payment = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_start = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_status = self.WS_LOAN_STATUS
        # WRITE loan_record FROM ws_loan_record. (Implementation depends on file handling)
        print(f"Writing loan record: {self.WS_LOAN_RECORD}")

    def disburse_funds_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10760-disburse_funds."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.process_deposit_C7()
        self.write_audit_trail_C7()

    def send_confirmation_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10770-send_confirmation."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_confirm'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Your loan has been approved'
        self.send_notification_C7()

    def process_decline_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10800-process_decline."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'DECLINED'
        self.record_decline_C7()
        self.send_decline_notice_C7()

    def record_decline_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10810-record_decline."""
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WSDeclineRecordC7()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_loan_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_status = self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_reason = self.WS_CONDITIONS
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_date = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # FUNCTION current_date
        # WRITE decline_record FROM ws_decline_record (Implementation depends on file handling)
        print(f"Writing decline record: {self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD}")

    def send_decline_notice_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10820-send_decline_notice."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_decline'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'LETTER'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your loan application'
        self.send_notification_C7()

    def portfolio_management_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11000-portfolio_management."""
        self.load_portfolio_C7()
        self.update_market_prices_C7()
        self.calculate_values_C7()
        self.rebalance_check_C7()
        self.generate_statements_C7()

    def load_portfolio_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11100-load_portfolio."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 11200-update_market_prices."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.HOLD_SYMBOL[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1]
            self.get_quote_C7()
            self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1] = self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def get_quote_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11250-get_quote."""
        self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol = self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
        # CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING quote_request quote_response (Implementation depends on external API)
        # Simulated quote retrieval
        if self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol == 'XYZ':
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'OK'
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("123.45")

def calculate_match_score_C9(ws_ofac_score: int, ws_pep_score: int, ws_match_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:"""
    """16120-calculate_match_score"""
    """
    if ws_ofac_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits if ws_watchlist_hits != 0 else Decimal('0')
    return ws_match_score"""
""""""
def determine_disposition_C9(ws_match_score: Decimal, ws_match_type: str, ws_sar_required: str, ws_case_status: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:"""
    """16130-determine_disposition"""
    """
    match ws_match_score:
        case score if score >= 90:
            ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
            ws_sar_required = 'Y'
        case score if score >= 75:
            ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
            ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
        case score if score >= 50:
            ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
        case _:
            ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status"""

def kyc_verification_C9(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str, ws_id_status: str, ws_addr_status: str, ws_doc_status: str, ws_kyc_status: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:"""

    return ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status, ws_kyc_status"""

def verify_identity_C9(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str) -> str:"""
    """16210-verify_identity"""
    """
    id_verify_ssn = ws_customer_ssn
    id_verify_dob = ws_customer_dob
    id_verify_name = ws_customer_name
    id_request = IDRequest()
    id_response = IDResponse()
    # CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING id_request id_response
    # Assume IDVERIFY is a function that takes IDRequest and returns IDResponse
    id_response = idverify(id_request)  # type: ignore
    id_verified = 'Y' # Assuming IDVERIFY populates a field called id_verified in ID_RESPONSE
    if id_verified == 'Y':
        ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_id_status = 'FAILED'
    return ws_id_status"""

def transaction_monitoring_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: int, ws_amount_threshold: int, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: int, ws_fraud_decision: str, ws_manual_review: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:"""
    """16400-transaction_monitoring"""
    """
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_velocity_C9(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_patterns_C9(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_high_risk_C9(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review = calculate_risk_score_C9(ws_fraud_score)
    return ws_fraud_score, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review"""
""""""
def check_velocity_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: int, ws_amount_threshold: int, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str, int]:"""
    """16410-check_velocity"""
    """
    ws_velocity_flag = 'N'
    ws_amount_flag = 'N'
    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score"""
""""""
def check_patterns_C9(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, int]:"""
    """16420-check_patterns"""
    """
    ws_pattern_flag = 'N'
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 15
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 30

def calculate_risk_score_C9(ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:"""
    """16440-calculate_risk_score"""
    """

    ws_manual_review = 'N'

    match ws_fraud_score:
        case score if score >= 80:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'
        case score if score >= 60:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'

            ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
        case _:

    return ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review"""

def generate_case_id_C9() -> str:
    """17110-generate_case_id"""
    """"""
    ws_date_part = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d") #FUNCTION current_date
    ws_random_part = int(random() * 99999) # type: ignore
    ws_case_id = f'CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}'
    return ws_case_id

def categorize_case_C9(ws_case_type: str, ws_open_date: str, ws_case_priority: int, ws_target_date: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """17120-categorize_case"""
    """

    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 2
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 3
        case _:"""
            ws_case_priority = 3"""
""""""
    ws_target_date = int(datetime.strptime(ws_open_date, "%Y%m%d").toordinal()) + ws_case_priority * 2
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def function_integer_of_date(date: str) -> int:
    """Dummy function for FUNCTION integer_of_date"""
    return int(date.replace("-", ""))

def function_random() -> float:
    """Dummy function for FUNCTION RANDOM"""
    return 0.5

    """Dummy function for FUNCTION MOD"""
    return a % b

def next_run_date(ws_last_run_date: str, run_frequency: str) -> int:
    """COBOL: Evaluate Statement"""
    match run_frequency:
        case 'DAILY':
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 1
        case 'WEEKLY':
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 365
        case _:
            ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date)
    return ws_next_run_date

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    ofac_search_result = ofacsrch_C11(OfacRequest(), OfacResponse())  # Simulate CALL 'OFACSRCH'
    OFAC_MATCH_FOUND = ofac_search_result[0]
    OFAC_MATCH_SCORE = ofac_search_result[1]

    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'

    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    ofac_search_result = ofacsrch_C11(OfacRequest(), OfacResponse())  # Simulate CALL 'OFACSRCH'
    OFAC_MATCH_FOUND = ofac_search_result[0]
    OFAC_MATCH_SCORE = ofac_search_result[1]
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'

        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    ach_input_file = AchInputFile()
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        ach_entry_result = read_ach_entry_C11(ach_input_file)  # Simulate reading ach_input_file
        if ach_entry_result is None: # Simulates AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = ach_entry_result  #Simulates READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
            validate_single_entry_C11()

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_ach_entry_C11(ach_input_file: AchInputFile) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, str] | None:
    """Placeholder for READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry. Returns None to signify end of file."""
    # Simulate reading the ACH input file.  In a real implementation, this would read from the file
    # For now, let's return some dummy data, and simulate the end of the file after a few reads'
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    if WS_VALID_ENTRIES + WS_INVALID_ENTRIES < 5:
        return ('123456789', '1234567890', Decimal(100.00), '22')  # Sample data
    else:
        return None  # Simulate end of file

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, ACH_ROUTING, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isdigit():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS = None  # TODO

    """Data for ws_funding_record"""
    funding_merchant: str = ""
    funding_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    funding_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    funding_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class WsSettleHeaderC12:
    """Data for ws_settle_header"""
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_merchant_id: str = ""
    settle_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class WsSettleDetailC12:
    """Data for ws_settle_detail"""
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_card: str = ""
    settle_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    settle_auth_code: int = 0

@dataclass
class WsSettleTrailerC12:
    """Data for ws_settle_trailer"""
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_total_count: int = 0
    settle_total_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class WsChargebackRecordC12:
    """Data for ws_chargeback_record"""
    cb_card: str = ""
    cb_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cb_reason: str = ""
    cb_case_id: str = ""
    cb_received_date: int = 0
    cb_status: str = ""

@dataclass
class WsFileErrorLogC12:
    """Data for ws_file_error_log"""
    file_err_name: str = ""
    file_err_status: str = ""

# Dummy functions/variables for external calls/files
def CVVVERIFY(card_number: str, cvv: str) -> str:
    """Dummy CVVVERIFY function"""
    return "M" if cvv == "123" else "N"

def FRAUDCHECK(auth_request: str) -> dict:
    """Dummy FRAUDCHECK function"""
    return {"fraud_score": 60, "fraud_decline_code": "05"}

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """29100-validate_stop_request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID, WS_STOP_REJECT
    WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order_C12() -> None:
    """29200-create_stop_order."""
    global WS_STOP_RECORD
    global ACCT_ID, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_CHECK_AMOUNT, WS_PAYEE_NAME, WS_PROCESS_DATE

    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecordC12()
    ws_stop_record.stop_account  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_NUMBER
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee  = None  # TODO: was WS_PAYEE_NAME
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = int(datetime.datetime.strptime(str(WS_PROCESS_DATE), '%Y%m%d').toordinal()) + 1721425 + 180
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = 'A'
    WS_STOP_RECORD = ws_stop_record

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """29300-apply_stop_fee."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CHECK_NUMBER
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
    update_account_C12()
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'stop_payment'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
    send_notification_C12()

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """30000-safe_deposit_box."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """30100-box_rental."""
    global WS_RENTAL_REQUEST
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """30110-check_availability."""
    global WS_BOX_AVAILABLE, WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_SIZE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
    WS_BOX_IDX = 0
    while True:
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1
        if WS_BOX_IDX > WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            break
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
            if BOX_SIZE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_IDX
                break

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """30120-assign_box."""
    global WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, BOX_STATUS, BOX_RENTER, BOX_RENTAL_DATE, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    BOX_STATUS[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
    BOX_RENTER[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    BOX_RENTAL_DATE[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """30130-create_rental_agreement."""
    global WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT
    global WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE
    ws_rental_agreement = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_box_number  = None  # TODO: was WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_customer  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_start_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_annual_fee = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[WS_REQUESTED_SIZE]
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = ws_rental_agreement

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """30200-box_access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_REQUEST
    if WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        if WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """30210-verify_renter."""
    global WS_RENTER_VERIFIED, BOX_RENTER, WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_ID_VERIFIED, WS_KEY_VERIFIED
    WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
    if BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
        if WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """30220-log_access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_LOG
    global WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_access_log = WsAccessLogC12()
    ws_access_log.access_box_number  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    ws_access_log.access_customer  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    ws_access_log.access_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_access_log.access_time = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    ws_access_log.access_type = 'ENTRY'
    WS_ACCESS_LOG = ws_access_log

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """30230-escort_to_vault."""
    global WS_DISPLAY_MSG
    WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """30300-box_drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_REQUEST
    if WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """30310-validate_drilling_auth."""
    global WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED, WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS, WS_COURT_ORDER, WS_DECEASED_RENTER, WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED
    WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
    if WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
        if WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """30320-schedule_drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_RECORD
    global WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_DRILLING_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_drilling_record = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    ws_drilling_record.drill_box_number  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    ws_drilling_record.drill_reason  = None  # TODO: was WS_DRILLING_REASON
    ws_drilling_record.drill_scheduled_date = int(datetime.datetime.strptime(str(WS_PROCESS_DATE), '%Y%m%d').toordinal()) + 1721425 + 30
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD = ws_drilling_record

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """30330-notify_renter."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'box_drilling'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """30400-box_billing."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_RENEWAL_DUE
    WS_BOX_IDX = 0
    while True:
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1
        if WS_BOX_IDX > WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            break
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
            if BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12()

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """30410-charge_annual_fee."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, BOX_RENTER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_FEE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_FEE_AMOUNT
    update_account_C12()
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """31000-merchant_services."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """31100-process_authorization."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE
    validate_card_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """31110-validate_card."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID
    WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """31115-check_luhn."""
    global WS_LUHN_SUM, WS_LUHN_IDX, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_LUHN_DIGIT, WS_LUHN_VALID
    WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
    WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
    while WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
        WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
        if (17 - WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
            WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
            if WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM += None  # TODO: was WS_LUHN_DIGIT
        WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1
    if WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """31116-check_expiry."""
    global WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_NOT_EXPIRED
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= WS_PROCESS_DATE:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """31117-check_cvv."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV, WS_CVV_RESULT, WS_CVV_VALID
    WS_CVV_RESULT = CVVVERIFY(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV)
    if WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """31120-check_fraud_score."""
    global WS_AUTH_REQUEST, WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE, FRAUD_SCORE, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE = FRAUDCHECK(WS_AUTH_REQUEST)
    FRAUD_SCORE = WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE["fraud_score"]
    if FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE["fraud_decline_code"]

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """31130-check_available_credit."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = {"available_credit": WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT} # Assuming a dictionary structure for file data
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT = WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC["available_credit"]
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

def approve_auth_C12() -> None:
    """31140-approve_auth."""
    global WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
    generate_auth_code_C12()
    global WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    record_authorization_C12()

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """31145-generate_auth_code."""
    global WS_AUTH_CODE, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
    WS_AUTH_CODE = int(random.random() * 999999)
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CODE

def record_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """31146-record_authorization."""
    global WS_AUTH_RECORD
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_MERCHANT_ID
    ws_auth_record = WsAuthRecordC12()
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_card  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_code = WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_time = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_merchant  = None  # TODO: was WS_MERCHANT_ID
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_status = 'P'
    WS_AUTH_RECORD = ws_auth_record

def decline_auth_C12() -> None:
    """31150-decline_auth."""
    global WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE, WS_DECLINE_RECORD
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    ws_decline_record = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_card  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_code = WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_DECLINE_RECORD = ws_decline_record

def capture_transaction_C12() -> None:
    """31200-capture_transaction."""
    global WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST
    if WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
        validate_auth_code_C12()
        if WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
            create_capture_record_C12()

def validate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """31210-validate_auth_code."""
    global WS_AUTH_VALID, WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE, AUTH_SEARCH_KEY, WS_AUTH_REC
    WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    #Simulate file read - replace with actual file read
    WS_AUTH_REC = {"auth_rec_status": 'P'} # Assuming a dictionary structure for file data
    if WS_AUTH_REC == {}:
        WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    else:
        if WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_status"] == 'P':
            WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'

def create_capture_record_C12() -> None:
    """31220-create_capture_record."""
    global WS_AUTH_REC, WS_CAPTURE_RECORD
    global WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT, WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_status"] = 'C' # Simulate rewrite
    ws_capture_record = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    ws_capture_record.capture_card = WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_card"] if "auth_rec_card" in WS_AUTH_REC else ''
    ws_capture_record.capture_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
    ws_capture_record.capture_auth_code = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    ws_capture_record.capture_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = ws_capture_record

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """31300-process_settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """31310-batch_transactions."""
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL, WS_BATCH_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CAPTURE_REC, CAPTURE_SETTLED, WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        WS_CAPTURE_REC = {"capture_settled": CAPTURE_SETTLED, "capture_amount": WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT}
        # Simulate file read, replace with actual file read
        if WS_CAPTURE_REC == {}: #Simulate EOF
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if WS_CAPTURE_REC["capture_settled"] == 'N':
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL += WS_CAPTURE_REC["capture_amount"]
                WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                CAPTURE_SETTLED = None  # TODO

    """

    risk_weighted_assets_C14(ws_cash_position, ws_govt_securities, ws_bank_deposits, ws_residential_mortgages, ws_commercial_loans, ws_consumer_loans, ws_trading_assets, ws_market_risk_factor, ws_gross_income, ws_operational_factor)
    capital_planning_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio, ws_total_capital, ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity, plan_recommended_action, plan_gap_amount, capital_plan_record, ws_capital_plan)
    stress_testing_C14(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_min_capital_ratio, ws_stress_pass_fail, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
"""
# 34100-calculate_capital_ratios"""
def calculate_capital_ratios_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal, ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34100-calculate_capital_ratios"""
    """
    calculate_tier1_C14(ws_common_stock, ws_retained_earnings, ws_aoci, ws_goodwill, ws_intangibles, ws_dta_deduction)
    calculate_tier2_C14(ws_sub_debt, ws_alll_eligible)
    calculate_ratios_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_total_assets)
"""
# 34110-calculate_tier1"""
def calculate_tier1_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34110-calculate_tier1"""
    """
    global ws_tier1_capital
    ws_tier1_capital = Decimal(0)
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction
"""
# 34120-calculate_tier2"""
def calculate_tier2_C14(ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34120-calculate_tier2"""
    """
    global ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital
    ws_tier2_capital = Decimal(0)
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible
    ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital
"""
# 34130-calculate_ratios"""
def calculate_ratios_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34130-calculate_ratios"""
    """
    global ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio
    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
        ws_cet1_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
        ws_capital_ratio = (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_leverage_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100
"""
# 34200-risk_weighted_assets"""
def risk_weighted_assets_C14(ws_cash_position: Decimal, ws_govt_securities: Decimal, ws_bank_deposits: Decimal, ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal, ws_commercial_loans: Decimal, ws_consumer_loans: Decimal, ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal, ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34200-risk_weighted_assets"""
    """
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = Decimal(0)
    credit_rwa_C14(ws_cash_position, ws_govt_securities, ws_bank_deposits, ws_residential_mortgages, ws_commercial_loans, ws_consumer_loans)
    market_rwa_C14(ws_trading_assets, ws_market_risk_factor)
    operational_rwa_C14(ws_gross_income, ws_operational_factor)
"""
# 34210-credit_rwa"""
def credit_rwa_C14(ws_cash_position: Decimal, ws_govt_securities: Decimal, ws_bank_deposits: Decimal, ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal, ws_commercial_loans: Decimal, ws_consumer_loans: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34210-credit_rwa"""
    """
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_cash_rwa, ws_govt_rwa, ws_bank_rwa, ws_mortgage_rwa, ws_commercial_rwa, ws_consumer_rwa
    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * Decimal('0.00')
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * Decimal('0.00')
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * Decimal('0.20')
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal('0.50')
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * Decimal('1.00')
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * Decimal('1.00')
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa
"""
# 34220-market_rwa"""
def market_rwa_C14(ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34220-market_rwa"""
    """
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_market_rwa
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa
"""
# 34230-operational_rwa"""
def operational_rwa_C14(ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34230-operational_rwa"""
    """
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_operational_rwa
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * Decimal('12.5')
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa
"""
# 34300-capital_planning"""
def capital_planning_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal, plan_recommended_action: str, plan_gap_amount: Decimal, capital_plan_record: object, ws_capital_plan: object) -> None:"""
    """34300-capital_planning"""
    """
    project_capital_needs_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio, ws_total_capital)
    identify_capital_actions_C14(ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity)
    update_capital_plan_C14(plan_recommended_action, plan_gap_amount, capital_plan_record, ws_capital_plan)
"""
# 34310-project_capital_needs"""
def project_capital_needs_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34310-project_capital_needs"""
    """
    global ws_projected_rwa, ws_required_capital, ws_capital_gap
    ws_projected_rwa = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
    ws_required_capital = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
    ws_capital_gap = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital
"""
# 34320-identify_capital_actions"""
def identify_capital_actions_C14(ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34320-identify_capital_actions"""
    """
    global ws_capital_action
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'
"""
# 34330-update_capital_plan"""
def update_capital_plan_C14(plan_recommended_action: str, plan_gap_amount: Decimal, capital_plan_record: object, ws_capital_plan: object) -> None:"""
    """34330-update_capital_plan"""
    """
    ws_plan_update_date = date.today().isoformat()
    plan_recommended_action = ws_capital_action
    plan_gap_amount = ws_capital_gap
    capital_plan_record = ws_capital_plan
    # Assuming REWRITE capital_plan_record FROM ws_capital_plan updates the record
    # This will require database interaction in Python

# 34440-compile_results"""
def compile_results_C14(ws_stress_pass_fail: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:"""
    """34440-compile_results"""
    """
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')

        remediation_actions_C14(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
"""
# 34450-calculate_stress_impact"""
def calculate_stress_impact_C14(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """34450-calculate_stress_impact"""
    """
    global ws_credit_losses, ws_market_losses, ws_stress_losses, ws_stressed_capital, ws_stressed_ratio, ws_stress_pass_fail
    ws_credit_losses = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    ws_market_losses = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / 100
    ws_stress_losses = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses
    ws_stressed_ratio = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'
"""

    balance_gl_C14(gl_master_file, ws_gl_record, ws_eof_flag, gl_asset, gl_liability, gl_equity, ws_error_msg)
    close_period_C14(ws_end_of_month, ws_process_date, ws_retained_earnings_acct, gl_master_file, ws_gl_record, gl_revenue, gl_expense, ws_period_close_rec, period_close_record, ws_eof_flag)

# 35100-post_journal_entry"""
def post_journal_entry_C14(ws_je_valid: str, ws_je_error: str, je_debit: list[Decimal], je_credit: list[Decimal], je_gl_account: list[str], gl_master_file: object, ws_gl_record: object, gl_account: str, gl_record: object, journal_record: object, ws_journal_entry: object) -> None:"""
    """35100-post_journal_entry"""
    """
    validate_journal_entry_C14(je_debit, je_credit, ws_je_valid, ws_je_error)
    if ws_je_valid == 'Y':
        post_to_accounts_C14(je_debit, je_credit, je_gl_account, gl_master_file, ws_gl_record, gl_account, gl_record)
        record_posting_C14(journal_record, ws_journal_entry)
"""
# 35110-validate_journal_entry"""
def validate_journal_entry_C14(je_debit: list[Decimal], je_credit: list[Decimal], ws_je_valid: str, ws_je_error: str) -> None:"""
    """35110-validate_journal_entry"""
    """
    global ws_total_debits, ws_total_credits
    ws_je_valid = 'Y'
    ws_total_debits = Decimal(0)
    ws_total_credits = Decimal(0)
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        ws_total_debits += je_debit[ws_je_idx - 1]
        ws_total_credits += je_credit[ws_je_idx - 1]
    if ws_total_debits != ws_total_credits:
        ws_je_valid = 'N'
        ws_je_error = 'OUT OF BALANCE'
"""
# 35120-post_to_accounts"""
def post_to_accounts_C14(je_debit: list[Decimal], je_credit: list[Decimal], je_gl_account: list[str], gl_master_file: object, ws_gl_record: object, gl_account: str, gl_record: object) -> None:"""
    """35120-post_to_accounts"""
    """
    global ws_gl_net_balance, ws_gl_debit_balance, ws_gl_credit_balance, ws_gl_account
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        if je_gl_account[ws_je_idx - 1] != ' ':
            ws_gl_account = je_gl_account[ws_je_idx - 1]
            # Assuming READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record KEY IS gl_account reads a record
            # Requires database interaction in Python
            # ws_gl_record = read_gl_record(gl_master_file, ws_gl_account)
            ws_gl_debit_balance += je_debit[ws_je_idx - 1]
            ws_gl_credit_balance += je_credit[ws_je_idx - 1]
            ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_debit_balance - ws_gl_credit_balance
            # Assuming REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record updates the record
            # Requires database interaction in Python
            # rewrite_gl_record(gl_record, ws_gl_record)
"""

    # Requires file or database interaction in Python
    # write_journal_record(journal_record, ws_journal_entry)

# 35200-balance_gl"""
def balance_gl_C14(gl_master_file: object, ws_gl_record: object, ws_eof_flag: str, gl_asset: bool, gl_liability: bool, gl_equity: bool, ws_error_msg: str) -> None:"""
    """35200-balance_gl"""
    """COBOL logic"""35300-close_period
    """
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        close_revenue_expense_C14(gl_master_file, ws_gl_record, gl_revenue, gl_expense, ws_eof_flag)
        update_retained_earnings_C14(ws_retained_earnings_acct, gl_master_file, ws_gl_record)
        record_close_C14(ws_process_date, ws_period_close_rec, period_close_record)
"""
# 35310-close_revenue_expense"""
def close_revenue_expense_C14(gl_master_file: object, ws_gl_record: object, gl_revenue: bool, gl_expense: bool, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:"""
    """35310-close_revenue_expense"""
    """
    global ws_net_income, ws_gl_debit_balance, ws_gl_credit_balance, ws_gl_net_balance
    ws_net_income = Decimal(0)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record reads a record
        # Requires database interaction in Python
        # ws_gl_record = read_gl_record(gl_master_file)
        # If end of file is reached during read, set ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        # Example implementation (replace with actual file reading logic):
        try:
            # ws_gl_record = next(gl_master_file_iterator)  # Assuming gl_master_file_iterator is an iterator
            pass # Remove after implementing DB
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            break

        if gl_revenue:
            ws_net_income += ws_gl_net_balance
            ws_gl_debit_balance = Decimal(0)
            ws_gl_credit_balance = Decimal(0)
            ws_gl_net_balance = Decimal(0)
            # Assuming REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record updates the record
            # Requires database interaction in Python
            # rewrite_gl_record(gl_record, ws_gl_record)

        if gl_expense:
            ws_net_income -= ws_gl_net_balance
            ws_gl_debit_balance = Decimal(0)
            ws_gl_credit_balance = Decimal(0)
            ws_gl_net_balance = Decimal(0)
            # Assuming REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record updates the record
            # Requires database interaction in Python
            # rewrite_gl_record(gl_record, ws_gl_record)

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
"""
# 35320-update_retained_earnings"""
def update_retained_earnings_C14(ws_retained_earnings_acct: str, gl_master_file: object, ws_gl_record: object) -> None:"""
    """35320-update_retained_earnings"""
    """
    global ws_gl_account, ws_gl_credit_balance, ws_gl_net_balance
    ws_gl_account = ws_retained_earnings_acct
    # Assuming READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record KEY IS gl_account reads a record
    # Requires database interaction in Python
    # ws_gl_record = read_gl_record(gl_master_file, ws_gl_account)
    ws_gl_credit_balance += ws_net_income
    ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_credit_balance - ws_gl_debit_balance
    # Assuming REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record updates the record
    # Requires database interaction in Python
    # rewrite_gl_record(gl_record, ws_gl_record)

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Assuming you have a function to read from intercompany_file
            ws_ic_balance = read_intercompany_file()
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAY.append(ws_ic_balance)  # Assuming ws_ic_balance is compatible
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT

    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF

    WS_SEARCH_FROM = IC_FROM_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX - 1]  # Adjust index to 0-based
    WS_SEARCH_TO = IC_TO_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX - 1]  # Adjust index to 0-based

    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if IC_FROM_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_TO:  # Adjust index to 0-based
            if IC_TO_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_FROM:  # Adjust index to 0-based
                WS_IC_DIFF = IC_AMOUNT[WS_IC_IDX - 1] + IC_AMOUNT[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1]  # Adjust index to 0-based
                if WS_IC_DIFF != 0:
                    log_ic_diff_C15()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1


def log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global IC_DIFF_RECORD, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF

    ws_ic_diff_rec = IntercompanyDiffRec()
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_FROM  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROM
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_TO  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TO
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_AMOUNT = Decimal(str(WS_IC_DIFF))
    IC_DIFF_RECORD = ws_ic_diff_rec

    # Assuming a file write operation here, replace with your actual implementation
    print(f"Writing to IC_DIFF_RECORD: {IC_DIFF_RECORD}")

def archive_audit_C15(WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, WS_TIMESTAMP):
    """38300-archive_audit"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_audit_record = read_audit_file()
            if WS_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                write_archive_audit_record(ws_audit_record)  # Assuming a function to write to archive_audit_record
                delete_audit_file()  # Assuming a function to delete from audit_file
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT

    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu()
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

def getcpu():
    """Placeholder for CALL 'GETCPU'"""
    return random.randint(70, 90)

def io_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME, WS_IO_THRESHOLD, WS_IO_ALERT

    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = getio()
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > WS_IO_THRESHOLD:
        WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y'

def getio():
    """Placeholder for CALL 'GETIO'"""
    return random.randint(5, 20)

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TPS, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME

    if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS != 0:
        WS_TPS = WS_TRANS_COUNT / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS
    else:
        WS_TPS = 0
    
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT != 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / WS_TRANS_COUNT
    else:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = 0


def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'
